## Journal objectives and implementation anchors

Objective labels come from the saved local journal export. They identify intent; the implementation transitions below establish graph evidence.

| Objective | Journal path |
| --- | --- |
| A clandestine meeting of reptilians at midnight? V, we're not missing this. If you want answers to the questions plaguing humankind for eons, this might be your only shot. Is there life in the cosmos? Is there anyone pulling the strings? If so, why? And why the fuck do they let all this evil shit happen? And to think the Universe's greatest secrets could be found in some old factory in our humble little Night City... (Primary) | `quests/minor_quest/mq026_conspiracy/stash/01_01_find_stash` |

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.
